# हिन्दी — Hindi / موطأ مالك — Muwatta of Imam Malik

## Narration 1351

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1365)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 31</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ مَسْعُودٍ، كَانَ يُحَدِّثُ أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ أَيُّمَا بَيِّعَيْنِ تَبَايَعَا فَالْقَوْلُ مَا قَالَ الْبَائِعُ أَوْ يَتَرَادَّانِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1365]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1365" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me that he had heard that Abdullah ibn Masud used to relate that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;When two parties dispute about a business transaction, the seller&#x27;s word is taken, or they make an agreement among themselves. Malik spoke about someone who sold goods to a man, and said at the contracting of the sale, &#x27;I will sell to you provided I consult so-and-so. If he is satisfied, the sale is permitted. If he dislikes it, there is no sale between us.&#x27; They made the transaction on that basis. Then the buyer regretted before the seller consulted the person. Malik said, &quot;That sale is binding on them according to what they described. The buyer has no right of withdrawal, and it is binding on him, if the person whom the seller stipulated to him, permits it.&quot; Malik said, &quot;The way of doing things among us about a man who buys goods from another and they differ about the price, and the seller says, &#x27;I sold them to you for ten dinars,&#x27; and the buyer says, &#x27;I bought them from you for five dinars,&#x27; is that it is said to the seller, &#x27;If you like, give them to the buyer for what he said. If you like, swear by Allah that you only sold your goods for what you said.&#x27; If he swears it is said to the buyer, &#x27;Either you take the goods for what the seller said, or you swear by Allah that you bought them only for what you said.&#x27; If he swears, he is free to return the goods. That is when each of them testifies against the other</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1365]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1365" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">इमाम मलिक ने रिवायत की कि अब्दुल्लाह इब्न मसूद (रा) बयां करते थे कि अल्लाह के रसूल (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: &#x27;जब दो फ़रीक़ किसी कारोबारी लेन देन में झगड़ा करें तो बेचने वाले की बात मानी जाये गई या वह आपस में समझौता कर लें.&#x27; इमाम मलिक ने उस शख्स के बारे में बताया जिस ने फरोख्त के वक़्त कहा: &#x27;मैं तुम्हें इस शर्त पर बेचता हूँ के मैं फलां से मशवरा कर लूं. अगर वह राज़ी हुआ तो सौदा जायज़ है हमारे दरमियान कोई सौदा नहीं.&#x27; अगर कीमत पर इख्तिलाफ हो तो बेचने वाले से कहा जाये गा के या तो खरीदार की बात मान लो या अल्लाह की कसम खाओ कि तुम ने इसी कीमत पर बेचा था.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1365]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1365" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">امام مالک نے روایت کی کہ عبداللہ بن مسعود رضی اللہ عنہ بیان کرتے تھے کہ اللہ کے رسول (صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم) نے فرمایا: &#x27;جب دو فریق کسی کاروباری لین دین میں جھگڑا کریں تو بیچنے والے کی بات مانی جائے گی یا وہ آپس میں سمجھوتہ کر لیں۔&#x27; امام مالک نے اس شخص کے بارے میں بتایا جس نے فروخت کے وقت کہا: &#x27;میں تمہیں اس شرط پر بیچتا ہوں کہ میں فلاں سے مشورہ کر لوں۔ اگر وہ راضی ہوا تو سودا جائز ہے، اگر اسے ناپسند ہوا تو ہمارے درمیان کوئی سودا نہیں۔&#x27; اگر قیمت پر اختلاف ہو تو بیچنے والے سے کہا جائے گا کہ یا تو خریدار کی بات مان لو یا اللہ کی قسم کھاؤ کہ تم نے اسی قیمت پر بیچا تھا۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1365]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1365" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Imam Malik ne riwayat ki ke Abdullah ibn Masud (RA) bayan karte the ke Allah ke Rasool (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: &#x27;Jab do fareeq kisi karobari lain dain mein jhagda karein to bechne wale ki baat maani jaye gi ya woh aapas mein samjhota kar lein.&#x27; Imam Malik ne us shakhs ke bare mein bataya jis ne farokht ke waqt kaha: &#x27;Main tumhein is shart par bechta hoon ke main falan se mashwara kar loon. Agar woh raazi hua to sauda jaiz hai, agar use napasand hua to hamare darmiyan koi sauda nahin.&#x27; Agar qeemat par ikhtilaf ho to bechne wale se kaha jaye ga ke ya to khareedar ki baat maan lo ya Allah ki qasam khao ke tum ne isi qeemat par becha tha.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1365]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1365" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 1352

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 31</strong></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ أَبِي الزِّنَادِ، عَنْ بُسْرِ بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، عَنْ عُبَيْدٍ أَبِي صَالِحٍ، مَوْلَى السَّفَّاحِ أَنَّهُ قَالَ بِعْتُ بَزًّا لِي مِنْ أَهْلِ دَارِ نَخْلَةَ إِلَى أَجَلٍ ثُمَّ أَرَدْتُ الْخُرُوجَ إِلَى الْكُوفَةِ فَعَرَضُوا عَلَىَّ أَنْ أَضَعَ عَنْهُمْ بَعْضَ الثَّمَنِ وَيَنْقُدُونِي فَسَأَلْتُ عَنْ ذَلِكَ زَيْدَ بْنَ ثَابِتٍ فَقَالَ لاَ آمُرُكَ أَنْ تَأْكُلَ هَذَا وَلاَ تُوكِلَهُ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1366" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Zayd ibn Thabit (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Abu&#x27;z-Zinad from Busr ibn Said from Ubayd, Abu Salih, the mawla of as-Saffah that he said, &quot;I sold drapery to the people of Dar Nakhla on credit. Then I wanted to go to Kufa, so they proposed that I reduce the price for them and they would pay me immediately . I asked Zayd ibn Thabit about that, and he said, &#x27;I order you not to accept increase or to give to anybody</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1366]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1366" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">अबु सालेह ने कहा मैंने अपना कपडा दार-इ-नखला वालों के हाथ वादे पर बेचा. जब मैं कूफ़ा जाने लगा तोह उन्होंने कहा कुछ कम कर दो तोह हम पैसे अभी दे देते हैं. मैंने ज़ैद बिन थाबित (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से पुछा तोह उन्होंने कहा मैं तुम्हे इस रुपये के खाने और खिलाने की इजाज़त नहीं देता.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1366" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">ابو صالح نے کہا میں نے اپنا کپڑا دار نخلہ والوں کے ہاتھ بیچا ایک وعدے پر جب میں کوفے جانے لگا تو ان لوگوں نے کہا اگر کچھ کم کردو تم تمہارا روپیہ ہم ابھی دے دیتے ہیں میں نے یہ زید بن ثابت سے بیان کیا انہوں نے کہا میں تجھے اس روپے کے کھانے اور کھلانے کی اجازت نہیں دیتا ۔</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Saleh ne kaha maine apna kapda daar-e-nakhla walon ke hath waade par becha. Jab main Kufa jane laga toh unhone kaha kuch kam kar do toh hum paise abhi de dete hain. Maine Zaid bin Thabit (Radi Allahu Anhu) se poocha toh unhone kaha main tumhe is rupaye ke khane aur khilane ki ijazat nahi deta.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1366" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

## Narration 1353

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1367)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 31</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1367]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1367" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1367</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1367 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ عُثْمَانَ بْنِ حَفْصِ بْنِ خَلْدَةَ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ سَالِمِ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ، أَنَّهُ سُئِلَ عَنِ الرَّجُلِ، يَكُونُ لَهُ الدَّيْنُ عَلَى الرَّجُلِ إِلَى أَجَلٍ فَيَضَعُ عَنْهُ صَاحِبُ الْحَقِّ وَيُعَجِّلُهُ الآخَرُ فَكَرِهَ ذَلِكَ عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ عُمَرَ وَنَهَى عَنْهُ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1367" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abdullah ibn Umar (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Uthman ibn Hafs ibn Khalda from Ibn Shihab from Salim ibn Abdullah that Abdullah ibn Umar was asked about a man who took a loan from another man for a set term. The creditor reduced the debt, and the man paid it immediately Abdullah ibn Umar disliked that, and forbade it</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1367]</p>

<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुल्लाह बिन उमर (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) से सवाल हुआ के एक शख्स का मियादी क़र्ज़ किसी पर हो और क़र्ज़दार कहे कुछ कम कर दो तो मैं नक़द दे देता हूँ, तो अब्दुल्लाह बिन उमर (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने इसको मकरूह जाना और मना किया।</p>

## Narration 1354

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1368)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 31</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1368]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1368" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1368</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1368 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنْ زَيْدِ بْنِ أَسْلَمَ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ كَانَ الرِّبَا فِي الْجَاهِلِيَّةِ أَنْ يَكُونَ لِلرَّجُلِ عَلَى الرَّجُلِ الْحَقُّ إِلَى أَجَلٍ فَإِذَا حَلَّ الأَجَلُ قَالَ أَتَقْضِي أَمْ تُرْبِي فَإِنْ قَضَى أَخَذَ وَإِلاَّ زَادَهُ فِي حَقِّهِ وَأَخَّرَ عَنْهُ فِي الأَجَلِ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَالأَمْرُ الْمَكْرُوهُ الَّذِي لاَ اخْتِلاَفَ فِيهِ عِنْدَنَا أَنْ يَكُونَ لِلرَّجُلِ عَلَى الرَّجُلِ الدَّيْنُ إِلَى أَجَلٍ فَيَضَعُ عَنْهُ الطَّالِبُ وَيُعَجِّلُهُ الْمَطْلُوبُ وَذَلِكَ عِنْدَنَا بِمَنْزِلَةِ الَّذِي يُؤَخِّرُ دَيْنَهُ بَعْدَ مَحِلِّهِ عَنْ غَرِيمِهِ وَيَزِيدُهُ الْغَرِيمُ فِي حَقِّهِ قَالَ فَهَذَا الرِّبَا بِعَيْنِهِ لاَ شَكَّ فِيهِ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ فِي الرَّجُلِ يَكُونُ لَهُ عَلَى الرَّجُلِ مِائَةُ دِينَارٍ إِلَى أَجَلٍ فَإِذَا حَلَّتْ قَالَ لَهُ الَّذِي عَلَيْهِ الدَّيْنُ بِعْنِي سِلْعَةً يَكُونُ ثَمَنُهَا مِائَةَ دِينَارٍ نَقْدًا بِمِائَةٍ وَخَمْسِينَ إِلَى أَجَلٍ هَذَا بَيْعٌ لاَ يَصْلُحُ وَلَمْ يَزَلْ أَهْلُ الْعِلْمِ يَنْهَوْنَ عَنْهُ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَإِنَّمَا كُرِهَ ذَلِكَ لأَنَّهُ إِنَّمَا يُعْطِيهِ ثَمَنَ مَا بَاعَهُ بِعَيْنِهِ وَيُؤَخِّرُ عَنْهُ الْمِائَةَ الأُولَى إِلَى الأَجَلِ الَّذِي ذَكَرَ لَهُ آخِرَ مَرَّةٍ وَيَزْدَادُ عَلَيْهِ خَمْسِينَ دِينَارًا فِي تَأْخِيرِهِ عَنْهُ فَهَذَا مَكْرُوهٌ وَلاَ يَصْلُحُ وَهُوَ أَيْضًا يُشْبِهُ حَدِيثَ زَيْدِ بْنِ أَسْلَمَ فِي بَيْعِ أَهْلِ الْجَاهِلِيَّةِ إِنَّهُمْ كَانُوا إِذَا حَلَّتْ دُيُونُهُمْ قَالُوا لِلَّذِي عَلَيْهِ الدَّيْنُ إِمَّا أَنْ تَقْضِيَ وَإِمَّا أَنْ تُرْبِيَ ‏.‏ فَإِنْ قَضَى أَخَذُوا وَإِلاَّ زَادُوهُمْ فِي حُقُوقِهِمْ وَزَادُوهُمْ فِي الأَجَلِ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1368" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: historical report. Attribution: Zayd ibn Aslam (successor). The source attributes a description of historical Jahiliyya transactions to Zayd ibn Aslam. Later compiler analogies and exposition remain context.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me that Zayd ibn Aslam said, &quot;Usury in the Jahiliyya was that a man would give a loan to a man for a set term. When the term was due, he would say, &#x27;Will you pay it off or increase me?&#x27; If the man paid, he took it. If not, he increased him in his debt and lengthened the term for him .&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1368]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1368" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1368</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1368 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said, &quot;The disapproved of way of doing things about which there is no dispute among us, is that a man should give a loan to a man for a term, and then the demander reduce it and the one from whom it is demanded pay it in advance. To us that is like someone who delays repaying his debt after it is due to his creditor and his creditor increases his debt.&quot; Malik said, &quot;This is nothing else but usury. No doubt about it.&quot; Malik spoke about a man who loaned one hundred dinars to a man for two terms. When it was due, the person who owed the debt said to him, &quot;Sell me some goods, whose price is one hundred dinars in cash for one hundred and fifty on credit.&quot; Malik said, &quot;This transaction is not good, and the people of knowledge still forbid it.&quot; Malik said, &quot;This is disapproved of because the creditor himself gives the debtor the price of what the man sells him, and he defers repayment of the hundred of the first transaction for the debtor for the term which is mentioned to him in the second transaction, and the debtor increases him with fifty dinars for his deferring him. That is disapproved of and it is not good. It also resembles the hadith of Zayd ibn Aslam about the transactions of the people of the Jahiliyya. When their debts were due, they said to the person with the debt, &#x27;Either you pay in full or you increase it.&#x27; If they paid, they took it, and if not they increased debtors in their debts, and extended the term for them</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1368" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">ज़ैद बिन असलम ने कहा जाहिलियत में सूद इस तरह होता था के मियाद गुज़रने पर क़र्ज़-ख्वाह कहता या क़र्ज़ अदा करो या सूद दो, अगर अदा ना कर पाता तो क़र्ज़ बढ़ा देता। मालिक ने कहा अगर किसी के 100 दिनार आते हों और मियाद पर वो कहे के 100 दिनार की चीज़ 150 दिनार में मियाद पर बेच दो, तो ये दुरुस्त नहीं क्योंकि ये सूद है और क़र्ज़ की मुद्दत बढ़ाने का एक हिला है।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1368" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">زید بن اسلم نے کہا کیا جاہلیت میں سود اس طور پر ہوتا تھا ایک شخص کا قرض میعادی دوسرے شخص پر آتا ہو جب میعاد گزر جائے تو قرضخواہ قرضدار سے کہے یا تم قرض ادا کرو یا سود دو اگر اس نے قرض ادا کیا تو بہتر ہے نہیں تو قرضخواہ اپنا قرضہ بڑھا دیتا اور پھر میعاد کراتا ۔ کہا مالک نے اگر کسی شخص کے دوسرے شخص پر سو دینار آتے ہوں وعدے پر جب وعدہ گزر جائے تو قرضدار قرضخواہ سے کہے تو میرے ہاتھ کوئی ایسی چیز جس کی قیمت سو دینار ہوں ڈیڑھ سو دینار کو بیع ڈال ایک میعاد پر یہ بیع درست نہیں اور ہمیشہ اہل علم اس سے منع کرتے رہے اس لئے کہ قرضخواہ نے اپنی چیز کی قیمت سو دینار وصول کرلی اور وہ جو سو دینار قرضے کے تھے ان کی میعاد بڑھا دی۔ بعوض پچاس دینار کے جو اس کو فائدہ حاصل ہو اس شئے کے بیچنے میں ۔ یہ بیع مشابہ ہے اس کے جو زید بن اسلم نے روایت کیا کہ جاہلیت کے زمانے میں جب قرض کی مدت گزر جاتی تو قرضخواہ قرضدار سے کہتا یا تو قرض ادا کر یا سود دے اگر وہ ادا کردیتا تو لے لیتا نہیں تو اور مہلت دے کر قرضہ کو بڑھا دیتا۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1368" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Zaid bin Aslam ne kaha Jahiliyat mein sood is tarah hota tha ke miyad guzarne par qarz-khwah kehta ya qarz ada karo ya sood do, agar ada na kar pata toh qarz badha deta. Malik ne kaha agar kisi ke 100 dinar aate hon aur miyad par wo kahe ke 100 dinar ki cheez 150 dinar mein miyad par bech do, toh ye durust nahi kyunki ye sood hai aur qarz ki muddat badhane ka ek hila hai.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1368" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

## Narration 1355

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1369)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 31</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ أَبِي الزِّنَادِ، عَنِ الأَعْرَجِ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ مَطْلُ الْغَنِيِّ ظُلْمٌ وَإِذَا أُتْبِعَ أَحَدُكُمْ عَلَى مَلِيءٍ فَلْيَتْبَعْ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1369]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1369" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Abu&#x27;z-Zinad from al Araj from Abu Hurayra that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;Delay in payment by a rich man is injustice, but when one of you is referred for payment to a wealthy man, let him be referred</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1369]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1369" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अबू हुरैरा (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फरमाया: मालदार शख्स का क़र्ज़ अदा करने में देर करना ज़ुल्म है, और जब तुम में से किसी का हवाला (ट्रांसफर) किसी मालदार शख्स पर किया जाए तो चाहिए के वो हवाला क़ुबूल कर ले।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1369]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1369" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ابوہریرہ رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا مالدار شخص کا دیر کرنا قرض ادا کرنے میں ظلم ہے اور جب تم میں سے کوئی حوالہ کیا جائے مالدار شخص پر تو چاہے کہ حوالہ قبول کرلے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1369]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Hurairah (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Maaldaar shakhs ka qarz ada karne mein der karna zulm hai, aur jab tum mein se kisi ka hawala (transfer) kisi maaldaar shakhs par kiya jaye toh chahiye ke wo hawala qubool kar le.</p>

## Narration 1356

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1370)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 31</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1370]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1370" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1370</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1370 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنْ مُوسَى بْنِ مَيْسَرَةَ، أَنَّهُ سَمِعَ رَجُلاً، يَسْأَلُ سَعِيدَ بْنَ الْمُسَيَّبِ فَقَالَ إِنِّي رَجُلٌ أَبِيعُ بِالدَّيْنِ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ سَعِيدٌ لاَ تَبِعْ إِلاَّ مَا آوَيْتَ إِلَى رَحْلِكَ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ فِي الَّذِي يَشْتَرِي السِّلْعَةَ مِنَ الرَّجُلِ عَلَى أَنْ يُوَفِّيَهُ تِلْكَ السِّلْعَةَ إِلَى أَجَلٍ مُسَمًّى إِمَّا لِسُوقٍ يَرْجُو نَفَاقَهَا فِيهِ وَإِمَّا لِحَاجَةٍ فِي ذَلِكَ الزَّمَانِ الَّذِي اشْتَرَطَ عَلَيْهِ ثُمَّ يُخْلِفُهُ الْبَائِعُ عَنْ ذَلِكَ الأَجَلِ فَيُرِيدُ الْمُشْتَرِي رَدَّ تِلْكَ السِّلْعَةِ عَلَى الْبَائِعِ إِنَّ ذَلِكَ لَيْسَ لِلْمُشْتَرِي وَإِنَّ الْبَيْعَ لاَزِمٌ لَهُ وَإِنَّ الْبَائِعَ لَوْ جَاءَ بِتِلْكَ السِّلْعَةِ قَبْلَ مَحِلِّ الأَجَلِ لَمْ يُكْرَهِ الْمُشْتَرِي عَلَى أَخْذِهَا ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ فِي الَّذِي يَشْتَرِي الطَّعَامَ فَيَكْتَالُهُ ثُمَّ يَأْتِيهِ مَنْ يَشْتَرِيهِ مِنْهُ فَيُخْبِرُ الَّذِي يَأْتِيهِ أَنَّهُ قَدِ اكْتَالَهُ لِنَفْسِهِ وَاسْتَوْفَاهُ فَيُرِيدُ الْمُبْتَاعُ أَنْ يُصَدِّقَهُ وَيَأْخُذَهُ بِكَيْلِهِ إِنَّ مَا بِيعَ عَلَى هَذِهِ الصِّفَةِ بِنَقْدٍ فَلاَ بَأْسَ بِهِ وَمَا بِيعَ عَلَى هَذِهِ الصِّفَةِ إِلَى أَجَلٍ فَإِنَّهُ مَكْرُوهٌ حَتَّى يَكْتَالَهُ الْمُشْتَرِي الآخَرُ لِنَفْسِهِ وَإِنَّمَا كُرِهَ الَّذِي إِلَى أَجَلٍ لأَنَّهُ ذَرِيعَةٌ إِلَى الرِّبَا وَتَخَوُّفٌ أَنْ يُدَارَ ذَلِكَ عَلَى هَذَا الْوَجْهِ بِغَيْرِ كَيْلٍ وَلاَ وَزْنٍ فَإِنْ كَانَ إِلَى أَجَلٍ فَهُوَ مَكْرُوهٌ وَلاَ اخْتِلاَفَ فِيهِ عِنْدَنَا ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ لاَ يَنْبَغِي أَنْ يُشْتَرَى دَيْنٌ عَلَى رَجُلٍ غَائِبٍ وَلاَ حَاضِرٍ إِلاَّ بِإِقْرَارٍ مِنَ الَّذِي عَلَيْهِ الدَّيْنُ وَلاَ عَلَى مَيِّتٍ وَإِنْ عَلِمَ الَّذِي تَرَكَ الْمَيِّتُ وَذَلِكَ أَنَّ اشْتِرَاءَ ذَلِكَ غَرَرٌ لاَ يُدْرَى أَيَتِمُّ أَمْ لاَ يَتِمُّ ‏.‏ قَالَ وَتَفْسِيرُ مَا كُرِهَ مِنْ ذَلِكَ أَنَّهُ إِذَا اشْتَرَى دَيْنًا عَلَى غَائِبٍ أَوْ مَيِّتٍ أَنَّهُ لاَ يُدْرَى مَا يَلْحَقُ الْمَيِّتَ مِنَ الدَّيْنِ الَّذِي لَمْ يُعْلَمْ بِهِ فَإِنْ لَحِقَ الْمَيِّتَ دَيْنٌ ذَهَبَ الثَّمَنُ الَّذِي أَعْطَى الْمُبْتَاعُ بَاطِلاً ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَفِي ذَلِكَ أَيْضًا عَيْبٌ آخَرُ أَنَّهُ اشْتَرَى شَيْئًا لَيْسَ بِمَضْمُونٍ لَهُ وَإِنْ لَمْ يَتِمَّ ذَهَبَ ثَمَنُهُ بَاطِلاً فَهَذَا غَرَرٌ لاَ يَصْلُحُ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَإِنَّمَا فُرِقَ بَيْنَ أَنْ لاَ يَبِيعَ الرَّجُلُ إِلاَّ مَا عِنْدَهُ وَأَنْ يُسَلِّفَ الرَّجُلُ فِي شَىْءٍ لَيْسَ عِنْدَهُ أَصْلُهُ أَنَّ صَاحِبَ الْعِينَةِ إِنَّمَا يَحْمِلُ ذَهَبَهُ الَّتِي يُرِيدُ أَنْ يَبْتَاعَ بِهَا فَيَقُولُ هَذِهِ عَشَرَةُ دَنَانِيرَ فَمَا تُرِيدُ أَنْ أَشْتَرِيَ لَكَ بِهَا فَكَأَنَّهُ يَبِيعُ عَشَرَةَ دَنَانِيرَ نَقْدًا بِخَمْسَةَ عَشَرَ دِينَارًا إِلَى أَجَلٍ فَلِهَذَا كُرِهَ هَذَا وَإِنَّمَا تِلْكَ الدُّخْلَةُ وَالدُّلْسَةُ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1370" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: Said ibn al-Musayyab (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Musa ibn Maysara that he heard a man ask Said ibn al-Musayyab, &quot;I am a man who sells for a debt.&quot; Said said, &quot;Do not sell except for what you take to your camel.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1370]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1370" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1370</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1370 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik spoke about a person who bought goods from a man provided that he provide him with those goods by a specific date, either in time for a market in which he hoped for their saleability, or to fulfil a need at the time he stipulated. Then the seller failed him about the date, and the buyer wanted to return those goods to the seller. Malik said, &quot;The buyer cannot do that, and the sale is binding on him. If the seller does bring the goods before the completion of the term, the buyer cannot be forced to take them.&quot; Malik spoke about a person who bought food and measured it. Then some one came to him to buy it and he told him that he had measured it for himself and taken it in full. The new buyer wanted to trust him and accept his measure. Malik said, &quot;Whatever is sold in this way for cash has no harm in it but whatever is sold in this way on delayed terms is disapproved of until the new buyer measures it out for himself. The sale with delayed terms is disapproved of because it leads to usury and it is feared that it will be circulated in this way without weight or measure. If the terms are delayed it is disapproved of and there is no disagreement about that with us.&quot; Malik said, &quot;One should not buy a debt owed by a man whether present or absent, without the confirmation of the one who owes the debt, nor should one buy a debt owed to a man by a dead person even if one knows what the deceased man has left. That is because to buy that is an uncertain transaction and one does not know whether the transaction will be completed or not completed.&quot; He said, &quot;The explanation of what is disapproved of in buying a debt owed by someone absent or dead, is that it is not known what unknown debtor may be connected to the dead person. If the dead person is liable for another debt, the price which the buyer gave on strength of the debt may become worthless.&quot; Malik said, &quot;There is another fault in that as well. He is buying something which is not guaranteed for him, and so if the deal is not completed, what he paid becomes worthless. This is an uncertain transaction and it is not good.&quot; Malik said, &quot;One distinguishes between a man who is only selling what he actually has and a man who is being paid in advance for something which is not yet in his possession. The man advancing the money brings his gold which he intends to buy with. The seller says, &#x27;This is 10 dinars. What do you want me to buy for you with it?&#x27; It is as if he sold 10 dinars cash for 15 dinars to be paid later. Because of this, it is disapproved of. It is something leading to usury and fraud</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1370" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">मूसा बिन मयसरा ने सुना के एक शख्स सईद बिन मुसैय्यब (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) से पूछ रहा था के मैं क़र्ज़ के बदले में बेचा करता हूँ, सईद ने कहा जो तेरे पास ना हो वो मत बेच। मालिक ने कहा जो शख्स कोई चीज़ इस शर्त पर ख़रीदे के वक़्त पर मिलेगी, और वो वक़्त पर ना मिले तो ख़रीदार को सौदा ख़त्म करने का हक़ नहीं, लेकिन अगर बेचने वाला वक़्त से पहले ले आए तो ख़रीदार मजबूर नहीं। मालिक ने मज़ीद कहा के दीन (उधार) को बेचना दुरुस्त नहीं क्योंकि इसमें धोखा है और ये वाज़ेह फरेब है।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1370" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1370]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1370" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Urdu · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1370</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1370 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">موسی بن میسرہ نے سنا ایک شخص پوچھ رہا تھا سعید بن مسیب سے میں قرض کے بدل میں بیچا کرتا ہوں سعید نے کہا تو نہ بیچ مگر اس چیز کو جو تیرے پاس ہو ۔ کہا مالک نے جو شخص کوئی چیز خرید کرے اس شرط پر کہ بائع (بچنے والا) وہ شئے مشتری (خریدنے والا) کو اتنی مدت میں سپرد کردے اس میں مشتری (خریدنے والا) نے کوئی مصلحت رکھی ہو مثلا اس وقت بازار میں اس مال کی نکاسی کی امید ہو یا اور کچھ غرض ہو پھر بائع (بچنے والا) اس وعدے میں خلاف کر اور مشتری (خریدنے والا) چاہے کہ وہ شئے بائع (بچنے والا) کو پھیر دے تو مشتری (خریدنے والا) کو یہ حق نہیں پہنچتا اور بیع لازم رہے گی اگر بائع (بچنے والا) اس شئے کو قبل میعاد کے لئے آیا تو مشتری (خریدنے والا) پر جبر نہ کیا جائے گا اس کے لینے پر۔ کہا مالک نے جو شخص اناج خرید کر اس کو تول لے پھر ایک خریدار آئے جو مشتری (خریدنے والا) سے اس اناج کو خرید کرنا چاہے مشتری (خریدنے والا) اس سے کہے کہ میں اناج تول چکا ہوں اور وہ شخص مشتری (خریدنے والا) کو سچا سمجھ کر اس غلے کو نقد مول لے لے تو کچھ قباحت نہیں مگر وعدے پر لینا مکروہ ہے جب تک وہ خریدار دوبارہ اس کو تول نہ لے۔ کہا مالک نے دین کا خریدنا درست نہیں خواہ غائب پر ہو یا حاضر پر مگر جب شخص حاضر اس کا اقرار کرے اسی طرح جو دین میت پر ہو اس کا بھی خریدنا درست نہیں کیونکہ اس میں دھوکا ہے معلوم نہیں وہ قرض ملتا ہے یا نہیں اس واسطے اگر میت یا غائب پر اور بھی دین نکلا تو اس کے پیسے مفت گئے دوسرے یہ کہ وہ قرض اس کی ضمان میں داخل نہیں ہو اگر نہ پنٹا تو اس کے پیسے مفت گئے۔ کہا مالک نے بیع سلف(قرض) میں اور بیع عینہ میں یہ فرق ہے کہ بیع عینہ والا دس دینار نقد دے کر پندرہ دینار وعدے پر لیتا ہے تو یہ صریح دھوکا ہے اور بالکل فریب ہے۔</p>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1370]</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Roman Urdu · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1370</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1370 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Musa bin Maysara ne suna ke ek shakhs Saeed bin Musayyib (Radi Allahu Anhu) se pooch raha tha ke main qarz ke badle mein becha karta hoon, Saeed ne kaha jo tere paas na ho wo mat bech. Malik ne kaha jo shakhs koi cheez is shart par khareede ke waqt par milegi, aur wo waqt par na mile toh khareedar ko sauda khatam karne ka haq nahi, lekin agar baichne wala waqt se pehle le aaye toh khareedar majboor nahi. Malik ne mazeed kaha ke deen (udhaar) ko bechna durust nahi kyunki ismein dhoka hai aur ye wazeh fareb hai.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1370" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1357

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1371)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 31</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ أَبِي بَكْرِ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ الْحَارِثِ بْنِ هِشَامٍ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ أَيُّمَا رَجُلٍ بَاعَ مَتَاعًا فَأَفْلَسَ الَّذِي ابْتَاعَهُ مِنْهُ وَلَمْ يَقْبِضِ الَّذِي بَاعَهُ مِنْ ثَمَنِهِ شَيْئًا فَوَجَدَهُ بِعَيْنِهِ فَهُوَ أَحَقُّ بِهِ وَإِنْ مَاتَ الَّذِي ابْتَاعَهُ فَصَاحِبُ الْمَتَاعِ فِيهِ أُسْوَةُ الْغُرَمَاءِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1371]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1371" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1371</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1371 ]</p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Malik said there was no harm if a man who sold some drapery and excluded some garments by their markings, stipulated that he chose the marked ones from that. If he did not stipulate that he would choose from them when he made the exclusion, I think that he is partner in the number of drapery goods which were purchased from him. That is because two garments can be alike in marking and be greatly different in price. Malik said, &quot;The way of doing things among us is that there is no harm in partnership, transferring responsibility to an agent, and revocation when dealing with food and other things, whether or not possession was taken, when the transaction is with cash, and there is no profit, loss, or deferment of price in it. If profit or loss or deferment of price from one of the two enters any of these transactions, it becomes sale which is made halal by what makes sale halal, and made haram by what makes sale haram, and it is not partnership, transferring responsibility to an agent, or revocation.&quot; Malik spoke about some one who bought drapery goods or slaves, and the sale was concluded, then a man asked him to be his partner and he agreed and the new partner paid the whole price to the seller and then something happened to the goods which removed them from their possession. Malik said, &quot;The new partner takes the price from the original partner and the original partner demands from the seller the whole price unless the original partner stipulated on the new partner during the sale and before the transaction with the seller was completed that the seller was responsible to him. If the transaction has ended and the seller has gone, the pre-condition of the original partner is void, and he has the responsibility.&quot; Malik spoke about a man who asked another man to buy certain goods to share between them, and he wanted the other man to pay for him and he would sell the goods for the other man. Malik said, &quot;That is not good. When he says, &#x27;Pay for me and I will sell it for you,&#x27; it becomes a loan which he makes to him in order that he sell it for him and if those goods are destroyed, or pass, the man who paid the price will demand from his partner what he put in for him. This is part of the advance which brings in profit.&quot; Malik said, &quot;If a man buys goods, and they are settled for him, and then a man says to him, &#x27;Share half of these goods with me, and I will sell them all for you,&#x27; that is halal, there is no harm in it. The explanation of that is that this is a new sale and he sells him half of the goods provided that he sells the whole lot.&quot; Yahya related to me from Malik from Ibn Shihab from Abu Bakr ibn Abd ar-Rahman ibn al-Harith ibn Hisham that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;Whenever a man sells wares and then the buyer becomes bankrupt and the seller has not taken any of the price and he finds some of his property intact with the buyer, he is more entitled to it than anyone else. If the buyer dies, then the seller is the same as other creditors with respect to it</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1371]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1371" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अबी बक्र बिन अब्दुर रहमान (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फरमाया: जिस शख्स ने अपना माल बेचा किसी के हाथ, फिर मुश्तरी (खरीदने वाला) मुफलिस हो गया और बाये (बेचने वाला) को समान (कीमत) वसूल नहीं हुई, लेकिन बाये ने अपनी चीज़ बह-ऐंसी मुश्तरी के पास पायी, तो बाये उस चीज़ का ज्यादा हकदार होगा। अगर मुश्तरी मर गया तो उस चीज़ में बाये और कर्ज़ख्वाहों के बराबर होगा।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1371]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1371" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ابی بکر بن عبدالرحمن رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ روایت ہے رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا جس شخص نے اپنا مال بیچا کسی کے ہاتھ پھر مشتری (خریدنے والا) مفلس ہو گیا اور بائع (بچنے والا) کو ثمن وصول نہیں ہوئی لیکن بائع (بچنے والا) نے اپنی چیز بجنسہ مشتری (خریدنے والا) کے پاس پائی تو بائع (بچنے والا) اس چیز کا زیادہ حقدار ہوگا اگر مشتری (خریدنے والا) مر گیا تو اس چیز میں بائع (بچنے والا) اور قرضخواہوں کے برابر ہوگا۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1371]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abi Bakr bin Abdur Rahman (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Jis shakhs ne apna maal becha kisi ke haath, phir mushtari (khareedne wala) muflis ho gaya aur baye (bechne wala) ko saman (keemat) wasool nahi hui, lekin baye ne apni cheez bah-ainsi mushtari ke paas payi, toh baye us cheez ka zyada haqdar hoga. Agar mushtari mar gaya toh us cheez mein baye aur qarzkhwahon ke barabar hoga.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1371]</p>

## Narration 1358

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1372)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 31</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، عَنْ أَبِي بَكْرِ بْنِ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ عَمْرِو بْنِ حَزْمٍ، عَنْ عُمَرَ بْنِ عَبْدِ الْعَزِيزِ، عَنْ أَبِي بَكْرِ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ الْحَارِثِ بْنِ هِشَامٍ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ أَيُّمَا رَجُلٍ أَفْلَسَ فَأَدْرَكَ الرَّجُلُ مَالَهُ بِعَيْنِهِ فَهُوَ أَحَقُّ بِهِ مِنْ غَيْرِهِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1372]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1372" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1372</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1372 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Yahya ibn Said from Abu Bakr ibn Muhammad ibn Amr ibn Hazm from Umar ibn Abdal-Aziz from Abu Bakr ibn Abd ar-Rahman ibn al-Harith ibn Hisham from Abu Hurayra that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;If anyone goes bankrupt, and a man finds his own property intact with him, he is more entitled to it than anyone else.&quot; Malik spoke about a man who sold a man wares, and the buyer went bankrupt. He said, &quot;The seller takes whatever of his goods he finds. If the buyer has sold some of them and distributed them, the seller of the wares is more entitled to them than the creditors. What the buyer has distributed does not prevent the seller from taking whatever of it he finds. It is the seller&#x27;s right if he has received any of the price from the buyer and he wants to return it to take what he finds of his wares, and in what he does not find, he is like the creditors.&quot; Malik spoke about some one who bought spun wool or a plot of land, and then did some work on it, like building a house on the plot of land or weaving the spun wool into cloth. Then he went bankrupt after he had bought it, and the original owner of the plot said, &quot;I will take the plot and whatever structure is on it.&quot; Malik said, &quot;That structure is not his. However, the plot and what is in it that the buyer has improved is appraised. Then one sees what the price of the plot is and how much of that value is the price of the structure. They are partners in that. The owner of the plot has as much as his portion, and the creditors have the amount of the portion of the structure.&quot; Malik said, &quot;The explanation of that is that the value of it all is fifteen hundred dirhams. The value of the plot is five hundred dirhams, and the value of the building is one thousand dirhams. The owner of the plot has a third, and the creditors have two-thirds.&quot; Malik said, &quot;It is like that with spinning and other things of the same nature in these circumstances and the buyer has a debt which he cannot pay. This is the behaviour in such cases.&quot; Malik said, &quot;As for goods which have been sold and which the buyer does not improve, but those goods sell well and have gone up in price, so their owner wants them and the creditors also want to seize them, then the creditors choose between giving the owner of the goods the price for which he sold them and not giving him any loss and surrendering his goods to him. &quot;If the price of the goods has gone down, the one who sold them has a choice. If he likes, he can take his goods and he has no claim to any of his debtor&#x27;s property, and that is his right. If he likes, he can be one of the creditors and take a portion of his due and not take his goods. That is up to him.&quot; Malik said about someone who bought a slave-girl or animal and she gave birth in his possession and the buyer went bankrupt, &quot;The slave-girl or the animal and the offspring belong to the seller unless the creditors desire it. In that case they give him his complete due and they take it</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1372]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1372" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अबू हुरैराह (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फरमाया: जिस शख्स ने अपना माल बेचा किसी के हाथ, फिर मुश्तरी मुफलिस हो गया और बाये ने अपनी चीज़ बह-ऐंसी मुश्तरी के पास पायी, तो वो उसका ज्यादा हकदार है। इमाम मालिक ने कहा: जिस शख्स ने कोई असबाब बेचा फिर मुश्तरी मुफलिस हो गया और बाये ने अपनी चीज़ बह-ऐंसी मुश्तरी के पास पायी, तो बाये उसको ले ले गा। अगर मुश्तरी ने उसमें से कुछ बेच डाला है, तो जिस कदर बाकी है उसका बाये ज्यादा हकदार है बनिस्बत और कर्ज़ख्वाहों के। अगर बाये थोड़ी सी समान पा चुका है, फिर बाये यह चाहे के उस समान को फेर कर जिस कदर असबाब अपना बाकी है उसको ले ले, और जो कुछ बाकी रह जाये उसमें वो और कर्ज़ख्वाह बराबर हैं, तो हो सकता है। इमाम मालिक ने कहा: अगर किसी शख्स ने सूत या ज़मीन खरीदी फिर सूत का कपड़ा बुन लिया और ज़मीन पर मकान बनाया, बाद उसके मुश्तरी मुफलिस हो गया, अब ज़मीन का बाये यह कहे के मैं ज़मीन और मकान सब लिये लेता हूँ तो यह नहीं हो सकता, बल्कि ज़मीन की और अमले (मटेरियल) की कीमत लगायेंगे, फिर देखेंगे इस कीमत का हिस्सा ज़मीन पर कितना आता है और अमले पर कितना आता है। अब बाये और मुश्तरी दोनों उसमें शरीक रहेंगे; ज़मीन का मालिक अपने हिस्से के मुवाफिक और बाकी कर्ज़ख्वाह अमले के मुवाफिक। इमाम मालिक ने कहा: इसकी मिसाल यह है जैसे ज़मीन और अमले की कीमत 1500 हुई, उसमें से ज़मीन की कीमत 500 है और अमले की 1000, तो ज़मीन वाले का 1/3 होगा और बाकी कर्ज़ख्वाहों के 2/3 होंगे। इमाम मालिक ने कहा: यही हुकुम सूत में है जबकि मुश्तरी ने उसको बुन लिया, बाद उसके कर्ज़दार हो कर मुफलिस हो गया। इमाम मालिक ने कहा: अगर मुश्तरी ने उस चीज़ में तसर्रुफ (यूज़) नहीं किया मगर उस चीज़ की कीमत बढ़ गयी, अब बाये यह चाहता है के अपनी शय फेर ले और कर्ज़ख्वाह चाहते हैं के वो शय बाये को न दें, तो कर्ज़ख्वाहों को इख्तियार है ख्वा बाये की समान पूरी पूरी हवाले कर दें। अगर उस चीज़ की कीमत घट गयी तो बाये को इख्तियार है, ख्वा अपनी चीज़ ले ले फिर उसको मुश्तरी के माल से कुछ गर्ज़ न होगी, ख्वा अपनी चीज़ न ले और कर्ज़ख्वाहों के साथ शरीक हो जाये। इमाम मालिक (रहमतुल्लाह अलैह) ने कहा: अगर किसी शख्स ने लौंडी खरीदी या जानवर खरीदा, फिर उस लौंडी या जानवर का मुश्तरी के पास आ कर बच्चा पैदा हुआ, बाद उसके मुश्तरी मुफलिस हो गया, तो वो बच्चा बाये का होगा। अलबत्ता अगर कर्ज़ख्वाह बाये की पूरी समान अदा कर दें, तो बच्चा को और उसकी माँ को दोनों को रख सकते हैं।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1372]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1372" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ابوہریرہ رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا جس شخص نے اپنا مال بیچا کسی کے ہاتھ پھر مشتری (خریدنے والا) مفلس ہو گیا اور بائع (بچنے والا) نے اپنی چیز بعینہ مشتری (خریدنے والا) کے پاس پائی تو وہ اس کا زیادہ حقدار ہے ۔ کہا مالک نے جس شخص نے کوئی اسباب بیچا پھر مشتری (خریدنے والا) مفلس ہوگیا اور بائع (بچنے والا) نے اپنی چیز بعینہ مشتری (خریدنے والا) کے پاس پائی تو بائع (بچنے والا) اس کو لے لے گا اگر مشتری (خریدنے والا) نے اس میں سے کچھ بیچ ڈالا ہے تو جس قدر باقی ہے اس کا بائع (بچنے والا) زیادہ حقدار ہے بہ نسبت اور قرضخواہوں کے ۔ اگر بائع (بچنے والا) تھوڑی سی ثمن پاچکا ہے پھر بائع (بچنے والا) یہ چاہے کہ اس ثمن کو پھیر کر جس قدر اسباب اپنا باقی ہے اس کو لے لے اور جو کچھ باقی رہ جائے اس میں اور قرضخواہوں کے برابر ہے تو ہو سکتا ہے۔ کہا مالک نے اگر کسی شخص نے سوت یا زمین خریدی پھر سوت کا کپڑا بن لیا اور زمین پر مکان بنایا بعد اس کے مشتری (خریدنے والا) مفلس ہوگیا اب زمین کا بائع (بچنے والا) یہ کہے کہ میں زمین اور مکان سب لئے لیتا ہوں تو یہ نہیں ہوسکتا بلکہ زمین کی اور عملے کی قیمت لگائیں کے پھر دیکھیں گے اس قیمت کا حصہ زمین پر کتنا آتا ہے اور عملے پر کتنا آتا ہے اب بائع (بچنے والا) اور مشتری (خریدنے والا اور بیچنے والا) دونوں اس میں شریک رہیں گے زمین کا مالک اپنے حصہ کے موافق اور باقی قرضخواہ عملے کے موافق ۔ کہا مالک نے اس کی مثال یہ ہے جیسے زمین اور عملے کی قیمت پندرہ سو ہوئی اس میں سے زمین کی قیمت پانچ سو ہے اور عملے کی ہزار ہے تو زمین والے کا ایک ثلث ہوگا اور باقی قرضخواہوں کے دو ثلث ہوں گے ۔ کہا مالک نے یہی حکم سوت میں ہے جب کہ مشتری (خریدنے والا) نے اس کو بن لیا بعد اس کے قرضدار ہو کر مفلس ہوگیا۔ کہا مالک نے اگر مشتری (خریدنے والا) نے اس چیز میں تصرف نہیں کیا مگر اس چیز کی قیمت بڑھ گئی اب بائع (بچنے والا) یہ چاہتا ہے کہ اپنی شئے پھیر لے اور قرضخواہ چاہتے ہیں کہ وہ شئے بائع (بچنے والا) کو نہ دیں گو قرضخواہ ہوں کو اختیار ہے خواہ بائع (بچنے والا) کی ثمن پوری پوری حوالے کردیں ۔ اگر اس چیز کی قیمت گھٹ گئی تو بائع (بچنے والا) کو اختیار ہے خواہ اپنی چیز لے لے پھر اس کو مشتری (خریدنے والا) کے مال سے کچھ غرض نہ ہوگی خواہ اپنی چیز نہ لے اور قرضخواہوں کے ساتھ شریک ہوجائے۔ کہا مالک رحمة اللہ علیہ نے اگر کسی شخص نے لونڈی خریدی یا جانور خریدا پھر اس لونڈی یا جانور کا مشتری (خریدنے والا) کے پاس آن کر بچہ پیدا ہوا بعد اس کے مشتری (خریدنے والا) مفلس ہوگیا تو وہ بچہ بائع (بچنے والا) ہوگا البتہ اگر قرضخواہ بائع (بچنے والا) کی پوری ثمن ادا کردیں تو بچہ کو اور اس کی ماں کو دونوں کو رکھ سکتے ہیں ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1372]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1372" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Hurairah (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Jis shakhs ne apna maal becha kisi ke haath, phir mushtari muflis ho gaya aur baye ne apni cheez bah-ainsi mushtari ke paas payi, toh woh uska zyada haqdar hai. Imam Malik ne kaha: Jis shakhs ne koi asbaab becha phir mushtari muflis ho gaya aur baye ne apni cheez bah-ainsi mushtari ke paas payi, toh baye usko le le ga. Agar mushtari ne usmein se kuch bech dala hai, toh jis qadar baqi hai uska baye zyada haqdar hai banisbat aur qarzkhwahon ke. Agar baye thodi si saman pa chuka hai, phir baye yeh chahe ke us saman ko pher kar jis qadar asbaab apna baqi hai usko le le, aur jo kuch baqi reh jaye usmein woh aur qarzkhwah barabar hain, toh ho sakta hai. Imam Malik ne kaha: Agar kisi shakhs ne soot ya zameen khareedi phir soot ka kapda bun liya aur zameen par makaan banaya, baad uske mushtari muflis ho gaya, ab zameen ka baye yeh kahe ke main zameen aur makaan sab liye leta hoon toh yeh nahi ho sakta, balki zameen ki aur amlay (material) ki keemat lagayenge, phir dekhenge is keemat ka hissa zameen par kitna aata hai aur amlay par kitna aata hai. Ab baye aur mushtari dono usmein shareek rahenge; zameen ka maalik apne hissay ke muwafiq aur baqi qarzkhwah amlay ke muwafiq. Imam Malik ne kaha: Iski misaal yeh hai jaise zameen aur amlay ki keemat 1500 hui, usmein se zameen ki keemat 500 hai aur amlay ki 1000, toh zameen wale ka 1/3 hoga aur baqi qarzkhwahon ke 2/3 honge. Imam Malik ne kaha: Yehi hukum soot mein hai jabke mushtari ne usko bun liya, baad uske qarzdaar ho kar muflis ho gaya. Imam Malik ne kaha: Agar mushtari ne us cheez mein tasarruf (use) nahi kiya magar us cheez ki keemat badh gayi, ab baye yeh chahta hai ke apni shay pher le aur qarzkhwah chahte hain ke woh shay baye ko na dein, toh qarzkhwahon ko ikhtiyar hai khwah baye ki saman poori poori hawalay kar dein. Agar us cheez ki keemat ghat gayi toh baye ko ikhtiyar hai, khwah apni cheez le le phir usko mushtari ke maal se kuch gharz na hogi, khwah apni cheez na le aur qarzkhwahon ke sath shareek ho jaye. Imam Malik (Rahmatullah Alaih) ne kaha: Agar kisi shakhs ne laundi khareedi ya jaanwar khareeda, phir us laundi ya jaanwar ka mushtari ke paas aa kar bacha paida hua, baad uske mushtari muflis ho gaya, toh woh bacha baye ka hoga. Albata agar qarzkhwah baye ki poori saman ada kar dein, toh bacha ko aur uski maa ko dono ko rakh sakte hain.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1372]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1372" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 1359

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1373)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 31</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ زَيْدِ بْنِ أَسْلَمَ، عَنْ عَطَاءِ بْنِ يَسَارٍ، عَنْ أَبِي رَافِعٍ، مَوْلَى رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَنَّهُ قَالَ اسْتَسْلَفَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم بَكْرًا فَجَاءَتْهُ إِبِلٌ مِنَ الصَّدَقَةِ قَالَ أَبُو رَافِعٍ فَأَمَرَنِي رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَنْ أَقْضِيَ الرَّجُلَ بَكْرَهُ فَقُلْتُ لَمْ أَجِدْ فِي الإِبِلِ إِلاَّ جَمَلاً خِيَارًا رَبَاعِيًا ‏.‏ فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏ &quot;‏ أَعْطِهِ إِيَّاهُ فَإِنَّ خِيَارَ النَّاسِ أَحْسَنُهُمْ قَضَاءً ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1373]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1373" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Zayd ibn Aslam from Ata ibn Yasar that Abu Rafi, the mawla of the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, borrowed a young camel and then the camels of sadaqa came to him.&quot; Abu Rafi said, &quot;He ordered me to repay the man his young camel. I said, &#x27;I can only find a good camel in its seventh year in the camels.&#x27; The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &#x27;Give it to him. The best of people are those who discharge their debts in the best manner</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1373]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1373" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अबू राफे&#x27; से रिवायत है जो मौला (आज़ाद किये हुए गुलाम) थे रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के, के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने कर्ज़ लिया एक छोटा ऊंट। जब सदक़े के वक़्त ऊंट आये और आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने मुझको हुकुम किया वैसा ही ऊंट अदा करने को, मैंने कहा: या रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम), सदक़े के ऊंटों में सब ऊंट अच्छे बड़े बड़े हैं, छह छह बरस के। आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फरमाया: इसमें से दे दे, अच्छे वो लोग हैं जो कर्ज़ अच्छे तौर पर अदा करें।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1373]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1373" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ابو رافع سے روایت ہے کہ جو مولیٰ (غلام آزاد کئے ہوئے) تھے رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے قرض لیا ایک چھوٹا اونٹ جب صدقے کے وقت کے وقت اونٹ آئے اور آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے مجھ کو حکم کیا ویسا ہی اونٹ ادا کرنے کو میں نے کہا یا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم صدقے کے اونٹوں میں سب اونٹ اچھے بڑے بڑے ہیں چھ چھ برس کے آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا اس میں سے دے دے اچھے وہ لوگ ہیں جو قرض اچھے طور پر ادا کریں۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1373]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Rafe&#x27; se riwayat hai jo mola (azad kiye hue ghulam) the Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke, ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne qarz liya ek chhota oont. Jab sadqay ke waqt oont aaye aur Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne mujhko hukum kiya waisa hi oont ada karne ko, maine kaha: Ya Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam), sadqay ke oonton mein sab oont achhe bade bade hain, chhe chhe baras ke. Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Ismein se de de, achhe woh log hain jo qarz achhe taur par ada karen.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1373]</p>

## Narration 1360

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1374)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 31</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنْ حُمَيْدِ بْنِ قَيْسٍ الْمَكِّيِّ، عَنْ مُجَاهِدٍ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ اسْتَسْلَفَ عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ عُمَرَ مِنْ رَجُلٍ دَرَاهِمَ ثُمَّ قَضَاهُ دَرَاهِمَ خَيْرًا مِنْهَا فَقَالَ الرَّجُلُ يَا أَبَا عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ هَذِهِ خَيْرٌ مِنْ دَرَاهِمِي الَّتِي أَسْلَفْتُكَ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ عُمَرَ قَدْ عَلِمْتُ وَلَكِنْ نَفْسِي بِذَلِكَ طَيِّبَةٌ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ لاَ بَأْسَ بِأَنْ يُقْبِضَ مَنْ أُسْلِفَ شَيْئًا مِنَ الذَّهَبِ أَوِ الْوَرِقِ أَوِ الطَّعَامِ أَوِ الْحَيَوَانِ مِمَّنْ أَسْلَفَهُ ذَلِكَ أَفْضَلَ مِمَّا أَسْلَفَهُ إِذَا لَمْ يَكُنْ ذَلِكَ عَلَى شَرْطٍ مِنْهُمَا أَوْ عَادَةٍ فَإِنْ كَانَ ذَلِكَ عَلَى شَرْطٍ أَوْ وَأْىٍ أَوْ عَادَةٍ فَذَلِكَ مَكْرُوهٌ وَلاَ خَيْرَ فِيهِ ‏.‏ قَالَ وَذَلِكَ أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَضَى جَمَلاً رَبَاعِيًا خِيَارًا مَكَانَ بَكْرٍ اسْتَسْلَفَهُ وَأَنَّ عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ عُمَرَ اسْتَسْلَفَ دَرَاهِمَ فَقَضَى خَيْرًا مِنْهَا فَإِنْ كَانَ ذَلِكَ عَلَى طِيبِ نَفْسٍ مِنَ الْمُسْتَسْلِفِ وَلَمْ يَكُنْ ذَلِكَ عَلَى شَرْطٍ وَلاَ وَأْىٍ وَلاَ عَادَةٍ كَانَ ذَلِكَ حَلاَلاً لاَ بَأْسَ بِهِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1374]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1374" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Humayd ibn Qays al-Makki that Mujahid said, &quot;Abdullah ibn Umar borrowed some dirhams from a man, then he discharged his debt with dirhams better than them. The man said, &#x27;Abu Abdar-Rahman. These are better than the dirhams which I lent you.&#x27; Abdullah ibn Umar said, &#x27;I know that. But I am happy with myself about that.&#x27; &quot; Malik said, &quot;There is no harm in a person who has borrowed gold, silver, food, or animals, taking to the person who lent it, something better than what he lent, when that is not a stipulation between them nor a custom. If that is by a stipulation or promise or custom, then it is disapproved, and there is no good in it.&quot; He said, &quot;That is because the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, discharged his debt with a good camel in its seventh year in place of a young camel which he borrowed, and Abdullah ibn Umar borrowed some dirhams, and repaid them with better ones. If that is from the goodness of the borrower, and it is not by a stipulation, promise, or custom, it is halal and there is no harm in it</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1374]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1374" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">मुजाहिद से रिवायत है के अब्दुल्लाह बिन उमर (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने एक शख्स से रुपये कर्ज़ लिये, फिर उस से अच्छे अदा किये। वो शख्स बोला: ऐ अब्दुर रहमान, यह तो मेरे रुपयों से अच्छे हैं। अब्दुल्लाह बिन उमर (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा: हाँ, मैं जानता हूँ, मगर मैंने अपनी खुशी से दिये हैं। इमाम मालिक ने कहा: जो शख्स सोना, चांदी या अनाज या जानवर कर्ज़ ले, फिर उस से बेहतर अदा करे तो कुछ कबाहत नहीं, जबकि उसकी शर्त न हुई हो या ऐसी रस्म न हो या उसका वादा न किया हो। अगर शर्त या रस्म या वादे के सबब से हो तो मकरूह है, बेहतर नहीं। इमाम मालिक ने कहा: देखो रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने छोटा ऊंट कर्ज़ ले कर अच्छा बड़ा ऊंट दिया और अब्दुल्लाह बिन उमर (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने रुपये कर्ज़ ले कर उस से ज्यादा दिये, मगर उसकी शर्त या वादा नहीं हुआ था, तो जो कोई खुशी से ऐसा करे हलाल है।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1374]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1374" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">مجاہد سے روایت ہے کہ عبداللہ بن عمر نے ایک شخص سے روپے قرض لئے پھر اس سے اچھے ادا کئے وہ شخص بولا اے عبدالرحمن یہ تو میرے روپوں سے اچھے ہیں عبداللہ بن عمر نے کہا ہاں میں جانتا ہوں مگر میں اپنی خوشی سے دئے ہیں ۔ کہا مالک نے جو شخص سونا چاندی یا اناج یا جانور قرض لے پھر اس سے بہتر ادا کرے تو کچھ قباحت نہیں جب کہ اس کی شرط نہ ہوئی ہو یا ایسی رسم نہ ہو یا اس کا وعدہ نہ کیا ہو اگر شرط یا رسم یاوعدے کے سبب سے ہو تو مکروہ ہے۔ بہتر نہیں ۔ کہا مالک نے دیکھو رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے چھوٹا اونٹ قرض لے کر اچھا بڑا اونٹ دیا اور عبداللہ بن عمر رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ نے روپے قرض لے کر اس سے بہت دیئے مگر اس کی شرط یا وعدہ نہیں ہوا تھا تو جو کوئی خوشی سے ایسا کرے حلال ہے۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1374]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1374" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Mujahid se riwayat hai ke Abdullah bin Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne ek shakhs se rupaye qarz liye, phir us se achhe ada kiye. Woh shakhs bola: Ae Abdur Rahman, yeh toh mere rupayon se achhe hain. Abdullah bin Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne kaha: Haan, main jaanta hoon, magar maine apni khushi se diye hain. Imam Malik ne kaha: Jo shakhs sona, chandi ya anaaj ya jaanwar qarz le, phir us se behtar ada kare toh kuch qabahat nahi, jabke uski shart na hui ho ya aisi rasm na ho ya uska wada na kiya ho. Agar shart ya rasm ya wade ke sabab se ho toh makrooh hai, behtar nahi. Imam Malik ne kaha: Dekho Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne chhota oont qarz le kar achha bada oont diya aur Abdullah bin Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne rupaye qarz le kar us se zyada diye, magar uski shart ya wada nahi hua tha, toh jo koi khushi se aisa kare halal hai.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1374]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1374" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ عُمَرَ بْنَ الْخَطَّابِ، قَالَ فِي رَجُلٍ أَسْلَفَ رَجُلاً طَعَامًا عَلَى أَنْ يُعْطِيَهُ إِيَّاهُ فِي بَلَدٍ آخَرَ فَكَرِهَ ذَلِكَ عُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ وَقَالَ فَأَيْنَ الْحَمْلُ يَعْنِي حُمْلاَنَهُ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1375" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Umar ibn al-Khattab (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that he had heard that Umar ibn al-Khattab said that he disapproved of one man lending another food on the provision that he gave it back to him in another city. He said, &quot;Where is the transport?</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1375]</p>

## Narration 1362

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1376)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 31</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1376]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1376" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1376</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1376 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ رَجُلاً، أَتَى عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ عُمَرَ فَقَالَ يَا أَبَا عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ إِنِّي أَسْلَفْتُ رَجُلاً سَلَفًا وَاشْتَرَطْتُ عَلَيْهِ أَفْضَلَ مِمَّا أَسْلَفْتُهُ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ عُمَرَ فَذَلِكَ الرِّبَا ‏.‏ قَالَ فَكَيْفَ تَأْمُرُنِي يَا أَبَا عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ فَقَالَ عَبْدُ اللَّهِ السَّلَفُ عَلَى ثَلاَثَةِ وُجُوهٍ سَلَفٌ تُسْلِفُهُ تُرِيدُ بِهِ وَجْهَ اللَّهِ فَلَكَ وَجْهُ اللَّهِ وَسَلَفٌ تُسْلِفُهُ تُرِيدُ بِهِ وَجْهَ صَاحِبِكَ فَلَكَ وَجْهُ صَاحِبِكَ وَسَلَفٌ تُسْلِفُهُ لِتَأْخُذَ خَبِيثًا بِطَيِّبٍ فَذَلِكَ الرِّبَا ‏.‏ قَالَ فَكَيْفَ تَأْمُرُنِي يَا أَبَا عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ قَالَ أَرَى أَنْ تَشُقَّ الصَّحِيفَةَ فَإِنْ أَعْطَاكَ مِثْلَ الَّذِي أَسْلَفْتَهُ قَبِلْتَهُ وَإِنْ أَعْطَاكَ دُونَ الَّذِي أَسْلَفْتَهُ فَأَخَذْتَهُ أُجِرْتَ وَإِنْ أَعْطَاكَ أَفْضَلَ مِمَّا أَسْلَفْتَهُ طَيِّبَةً بِهِ نَفْسُهُ فَذَلِكَ شُكْرٌ شَكَرَهُ لَكَ وَلَكَ أَجْرُ مَا أَنْظَرْتَهُ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1376" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abdullah ibn Umar (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">And Malik related to me that he had heard that a man came to Abdullah ibn Umar and said, &quot;Abu Abd ar-Rahman, I gave a man a loan and stipulated that he give me better than what I lent him.&quot; Abdullah ibn Umar said, &quot;That is usury.&quot; Abdullah said, &quot;Loans are of three types:A free loan which you lend by which you desire the pleasure of Allah, and so you have the pleasure of Allah. A free loan which you lend by which you desire the pleasure of your companion, so you have the pleasure of your companion, and a free loan which you lend by which you take what is impure by what is pure, and that is usury.&quot; He said, &quot;What do you order me to do, Abu Abd ar-Rahman?&quot; He said, &quot;I think that you should tear up the agreement. If he gives you the like of what you lent him, accept it. If he gives you less than what you lent him, take it and you will be rewarded. If he gives you better than what you lent him, of his own good will, that is his gratitude to you and you have the wage of the period you gave him the loan</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1376]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1376" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">हज़रत उमर बिन खत्ताब (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) से किसी ने कहा: जो शख्स किसी को अनाज कर्ज़ दिये इस शर्त पर के फुलाने शहर में अदा करना, उन्होंने इसको मकरूह जाना और कहा: बार बरदारी की उजरत कहाँ जायेगी? एक शख्स अब्दुल्लाह बिन उमर (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) के पास आया और कहा: मैंने एक शख्स को कर्ज़ दिया और उम्दा उस से ठहराया। अब्दुल्लाह बिन उमर (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा: यह रिबा है। उसने कहा: फिर क्या हुकुम करते हो? अब्दुल्लाह बिन उमर (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा: कर्ज़ तीन तौर पर है, एक अल्लाह के वास्ते इसमें तो अल्लाह की रज़ामंदी है, एक अपने दोस्त की खुशी के लिये इसमें दोस्त की रज़ामंदी है, एक कर्ज़ इस वास्ते है के हलाल माल दे कर हराम माल ले, यह सूद है। फिर वो शख्स बोला: अब मुझको क्या हुकुम करते हो अबू अब्दुर रहमान? उन्होंने कहा: मेरे नज़दीक मुनासिब यह है के तू दस्तवेज़ (डॉक्यूमेंट) को फाड़ डाल। अगर वो शख्स जिसको तू ने कर्ज़ दिया है जैसा माल तू ने दिया है वैसा ही दे तो ले ले, अगर उस से बुरा दे और तू ले ले तो तुझे अजर होगा, अगर वो अपनी खुशी से उस से अच्छा दे तो उसने तेरा शुक्रिया अदा किया और तूने जो इतने दिनों तक उसको मुहलत दी उसका सवाब तुझे मिला।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1376" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1376 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حضرت عمر بن خطابن سے کسی نے کہا جو شخص کسی کو اناج قرض دئیے اس شرط پر کہ فلانے شہر میں ادا کرنا انہوں نے اس کو مکروہ جانا اور کہا بار برداری کی اجرت کہاں جائے گی ۔ ایک شخص عبداللہ بن عمر کے پاس آیا اور کہا میں نے ایک شخص کو قرض دیا اور عمدہ اس سے ٹھہرایا عبداللہ بن عرمں نے کہا یہ ربا ہے اس نے کہا پھر کیا حکم کرتے ہو عبداللہ بن عمر نے کہا قرض تین طور پر ہے ایک اللہ کے واسطے اس میں تو اللہ کی رضا مندی ہے ایک اپنے دوست کی خوشی کے لئے اس میں دوست کی رضا مندی ہے ایک قرض اس واسطے ہے کہ حلال مال دے کر حرام مال لے یہ سود ہے پھر وہ شخص بولا اب مجھ کو کیا حکم کرتے ہو ابوعبدالرحمن انہوں نے کہا میرے نزدیک مناسب یہ ہے کہ تو دستاویز کو پھاڑ ڈال اگر وہ شخص جس کو تو نے قرض دیا ہے جیسا مال تو نے دیا ہے ویسا ہی دے تو لے لے اگر اس سے برا دے اور تو لے لے تو تجھے اجر ہوگا اگر وہ اپنی خوشی سے اس سے اچھا دے تو اس نے تیرا شکریہ ادا کیا اور تو نے جو اتنے دنوں تک اس کو مہلت دی اس کا ثواب تجھے ملا۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1376" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1376]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1376" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Roman Urdu · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1376</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1376 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Hazrat Umar bin Khattab (Radi Allahu Anhu) se kisi ne kaha: Jo shakhs kisi ko anaaj qarz diye is shart par ke fulane shehr mein ada karna, unhone isko makrooh jana aur kaha: Baar bardari ki ujrat kahan jayegi? Ek shakhs Abdullah bin Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ke paas aaya aur kaha: Maine ek shakhs ko qarz diya aur umdah us se thehraya. Abdullah bin Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne kaha: Yeh riba hai. Usne kaha: Phir kya hukum karte ho? Abdullah bin Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne kaha: Qarz teen taur par hai, ek Allah ke waste ismein toh Allah ki razamandi hai, ek apne dost ki khushi ke liye ismein dost ki razamandi hai, ek qarz is waste hai ke halal maal de kar haram maal le, yeh sood hai. Phir woh shakhs bola: Ab mujhko kya hukum karte ho Abu Abdur Rahman? Unhone kaha: Mere nazdeek munasib yeh hai ke tu dastaveez (document) ko phaad daal. Agar woh shakhs jisko tu ne qarz diya hai jaisa maal tu ne diya hai waisa hi de toh le le, agar us se bura de aur tu le le toh tujhe ajar hoga, agar woh apni khushi se us se achha de toh usne tera shukriya ada kiya aur tune jo itne dino tak usko muhlat di uska sawab tujhe mila.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1376" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1364

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 31</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ مَسْعُودٍ، كَانَ يَقُولُ مَنْ أَسْلَفَ سَلَفًا فَلاَ يَشْتَرِطْ أَفْضَلَ مِنْهُ وَإِنْ كَانَتْ قَبْضَةً مِنْ عَلَفٍ فَهُوَ رِبًا ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ الأَمْرُ الْمُجْتَمَعُ عَلَيْهِ عِنْدَنَا أَنَّ مَنِ اسْتَسْلَفَ شَيْئًا مِنَ الْحَيَوَانِ بِصِفَةٍ وَتَحْلِيَةٍ مَعْلُومَةٍ فَإِنَّهُ لاَ بَأْسَ بِذَلِكَ وَعَلَيْهِ أَنْ يَرُدَّ مِثْلَهُ إِلاَّ مَا كَانَ مِنَ الْوَلاَئِدِ فَإِنَّهُ يُخَافُ فِي ذَلِكَ الذَّرِيعَةُ إِلَى إِحْلاَلِ مَا لاَ يَحِلُّ فَلاَ يَصْلُحُ وَتَفْسِيرُ مَا كُرِهَ مِنْ ذَلِكَ أَنْ يَسْتَسْلِفَ الرَّجُلُ الْجَارِيَةَ فَيُصِيبُهَا مَا بَدَا لَهُ ثُمَّ يَرُدُّهَا إِلَى صَاحِبِهَا بِعَيْنِهَا فَذَلِكَ لاَ يَصْلُحُ وَلاَ يَحِلُّ وَلَمْ يَزَلْ أَهْلُ الْعِلْمِ يَنْهَوْنَ عَنْهُ وَلاَ يُرَخِّصُونَ فِيهِ لأَحَدٍ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1378" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abdullah ibn Masud (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me that he had heard that Abdullah ibn Masud used to say, &quot;If someone makes a loan, they should not stipulate better than it. Even if it is a handful of grass, it is usury.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1378]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1378" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1378</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1378 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said, &quot;The generally agreed on way of doing things among us is that there is no harm in borrowing any animals with a set description and itemisation, and one must return the like of them. This is not done in the case of female slaves. It is feared about that that it will lead to making halal what is not halal, so it is not good. The explanation of what is disapproved of in that, is that a man borrow a slave-girl and have intercourse with her as seems proper to him. Then he returns her to her owner. That is not good and it is not halal. The people of knowledge still forbid it and do not give an indulgence to any one in it</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1378" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुल्लाह बिन उम्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) कहते थे: जो शख्स किसी को क़र्ज़ दे तोह सिवाये क़र्ज़ अदा करने के और कोई शर्त न कराये. अब्दुल्लाह बिन मसूद (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) कहते थे: जो शख्स किसी को क़र्ज़ दे उस से ज़्यादा न ठहराये अगर एक मुट्ठी घास की हो. इमाम मलिक ने कहा: हमारे नज़दीक यह हुकुम इत्तेफ़ाक़ी है के जो शख्स कोई जानवर जिसका हुल्य और सिफ़त मालूम हो किसी को क़र्ज़ दे तोह कुछ क़बाहत नहीं दुरुस्त नहीं क्यूंकि यह जरिया है हराम के हलाल करने का; लोग एक दूसरे की लौंडी क़र्ज़ ले आयेंगे फिर जब तक जी चाहेगा उस से जमा&#x27; करेंगे बाद उसके मालिक को फेर देंगे और किसी को इसकी इजाज़त न दी.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1378" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">عبداللہ بن عمر کہتے تھے جو شخص کسی کو قرض دے تو سوائے قرض ادا کرنے کے اور کوئی شرط نہ کرائے ۔ عبداللہ بن مسعود کہتے تھے جو شخص کسی کو قرض دے اس سے زیادہ نہ ٹھہرائے اگر ایک مٹھی گھاس کی ہو ۔ کہا مالک نے ہمارے نزدیک یہ حکم اتفاقی ہے کہ جو شخص کوئی جانور جس کا حلیہ اور صفت معلوم ہو کسی کو قرض دے تو کچھ قباحت نہیں اب مقروض ویسا ہی جانور ادا کرے۔ مگر لونڈی کو قرض لینا درست نہیں کیونکہ یہ ذریعہ ہے حرام کے حلال کرنے کا لوگ ایک درسرے کی لونڈی قرض لے آئیں گے پھر جب تک جی چاہے گا اس سے جماع کریں گے بعد اس کے مالک کو پھیر دیں گے یہ تو حلال نہیں ہمیشہ اہل علم اس سے منع کرتے رہے اور کسی کو اس کی اجازت نہ دی۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1378" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Abdullah bin Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) kehte the: Jo shakhs kisi ko qarz de toh siwaye qarz ada karne ke aur koi shart na karaye. Abdullah bin Masood (Radi Allahu Anhu) kehte the: Jo shakhs kisi ko qarz de us se zyada na thehraye agar ek mutthi ghaas ki ho. Imam Malik ne kaha: Hamare nazdeek yeh hukum ittefaqi hai ke jo shakhs koi jaanwar jiska hulya aur sifat maloom ho kisi ko qarz de toh kuch qabahat nahi, ab maqrooz waisa hi jaanwar ada kare. Magar laundi ko qarz lena durust nahi kyunke yeh zariya hai haram ke halal karne ka; log ek dusre ki laundi qarz le aayenge phir jab tak jee chahega us se jama&#x27; karenge baad uske maalik ko pher denge, yeh toh halal nahi. Hamesha ahl-e-ilm is se mana karte rahe aur kisi ko iski ijazat na di.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1378" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1365

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1379)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 31</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ لاَ يَبِعْ بَعْضُكُمْ عَلَى بَيْعِ بَعْضٍ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1379]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1379" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Nafi from Abdullah ibn Umar that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;Do not let any of you bid against each other</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1379]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुल्लाह बिन उम्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के फ़रमाया रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने: न बेचें बाज़ तुम्हारे ऊपर बाज़ के (यानी एक की बेंच पर दूसरा बेच न करे).</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1379]</p>
